```diff
+            </div>
+        </section>
+    </section>
+</article>
+
+<script>
+(() => {
+    // 5년 이전 날짜 계산 (DatePicker minDate 제약 및 검증에 공통 사용)
+    const fiveYearsAgo = new Date();
+    fiveYearsAgo.setFullYear(fiveYearsAgo.getFullYear() - 5);
+    fiveYearsAgo.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
+
+    // DatePicker 초기화
+    const datePicker = new ncua.DatePicker(document.querySelector('#datepicker-container'), {
+        size: 'xs',
+        buttons: [
+            { text: '오늘',  period: 0,  unit: 'days', isCurrent: false },
+            { text: '7일',   period: 6,  unit: 'days', isCurrent: true  },
+            { text: '15일',  period: 14, unit: 'days', isCurrent: false },
+            { text: '1개월', period: 29, unit: 'days', isCurrent: false },
+            { text: '3개월', period: 89, unit: 'days', isCurrent: false },
+        ],
+        datePickerOptions: [
+            {
+                element: 'start-date',
+                attrName: 'orderDt[]',
+                options: { defaultDate: '<?= htmlspecialchars($orderDt[0]) ?>', mode: 'single', static: true, dateFormat: 'Y-m-d', clickOpens: true, allowInput: true, locale: 'ko', minDate: fiveYearsAgo, maxDate: 'today' },
+            },
+            {
+                element: 'end-date',
+                attrName: 'orderDt[]',
+                options: { defaultDate: '<?= htmlspecialchars($orderDt[1]) ?>', mode: 'single', static: true, dateFormat: 'Y-m-d', clickOpens: true, allowInput: true, locale: 'ko', minDate: fiveYearsAgo, maxDate: 'today' },
+            },
+        ],
+    });
+    datePicker.setDate(["<?= htmlspecialchars($orderDt[0]) ?>", "<?= htmlspecialchars($orderDt[1]) ?>"]);
+
+    // -------------------------------------------------------
+    // DatePicker 날짜 제약 검증
+    // - 출발일 > 종료일 불가
+    // - 5년 이전 날짜 설정 불가 (allowInput 직접 입력 우회 대비)
+    // -------------------------------------------------------
+    let prevDates = datePicker.getDates();
+
+    document.querySelector('#datepicker-container').addEventListener('change', (e) => {
+        if (!e.target.name?.includes('orderDt')) return;
+
+        const dates   = datePicker.getDates();
+        const startVal = dates[0];
+        const endVal   = dates[1];
+
+        // 하나라도 비어 있으면 유효 상태로 저장 후 종료
+        if (!startVal || !endVal) {
+            prevDates = dates;
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const startDate = new Date(startVal);
+        const endDate   = new Date(endVal);
+
+        // 출발일 > 종료일 차단
+        if (startDate > endDate) {
+            const inputs = document.querySelectorAll('#datepicker-container input[name="orderDt[]"]');
+            const isStartChanged = e.target === inputs[0];
+            NCDSAlert({
+                message: isStartChanged
+                    ? '시작일자는 종료일자보다 이후일 수 없습니다.'
+                    : '종료일자는 시작일자보다 이전일 수 없습니다.',
+                iconType: 'error',
+            });
+            datePicker.setDate(prevDates);
+            return;
+        }
+
+        prevDates = dates;
+    });
+
+    // 공급사 선택 행 show/hide
+    const scmSelectRow = document.getElementById('scmSelectRow');
+    const checkedScmFl = document.querySelector('.supply-radio-group input[name="scmFl"]:checked');
+    if (checkedScmFl?.value === 'y') {
+        scmSelectRow.classList.remove('scm-select-row');
+    }
+    document.querySelector('.supply-radio-group')?.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
+        if (e.target.name === 'scmFl') {
+            scmSelectRow.classList.toggle('scm-select-row', e.target.value !== 'y');
+        }
+    });
+
+    // 공급사 콤보박스 초기화
+    const initSupplyComboBoxHandler = () => {
+        if (typeof window.initSupplyComboBox === 'undefined') {
+            setTimeout(initSupplyComboBoxHandler, 100);
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const initData = [];
+        <?php if ($search['scmFl'] === 'y' && !empty($search['scmNo'])): ?>
+            <?php foreach ($search['scmNo'] as $k => $v): ?>
+                initData.push({ id: <?= json_encode((string)$v) ?>, label: <?= json_encode($search['scmNoNm'][$k] ?? '') ?> });
+            <?php endforeach; ?>
+        <?php endif; ?>
+
+        const applySupplyComboBox = window.initSupplyComboBox({
+            scmLayerSelector: 'scmLayer',
+            dataInputNm: 'scmNo',
+            comboBoxLayerId: 'ncua-combo-box-layer',
+            radioGroupClass: 'supply-radio-group',
+            initData: initData,
+            apiUrl: '../share/ncds/layer_scm.php',
+            tagIdPrefix: 'info_scm',
+            tagHiddenName: 'scmNoNm',
+        });
+
+        window.applySupplyComboBox = applySupplyComboBox;
+    };
+
+    initSupplyComboBoxHandler();
+
+    // -------------------------------------------------------
+    // 검색어 에러 표시 / 해제 헬퍼
+    // -------------------------------------------------------
+    const showSearchWordError = (input) => {
+        const wrapper = input.closest('.ncua-input');
+        wrapper.classList.add('destructive');
+        if (window.NCDSValidator) NCDSValidator.addErrorIcon(input);
+        let hint = wrapper.querySelector('.ncua-hint-text');
+        if (!hint) {
+            hint = document.createElement('span');
+            hint.className = 'ncua-hint-text ncua-input__hint-text';
+            wrapper.appendChild(hint);
+        }
+        hint.classList.add('destructive');
+        hint.textContent = '검색어를 입력하세요.';
+    };
+
+    const clearSearchWordError = (input) => {
+        const wrapper = input.closest('.ncua-input');
+        wrapper.classList.remove('destructive');
+        if (window.NCDSValidator) NCDSValidator.removeErrorIcon(input);
+        const hint = wrapper.querySelector('.ncua-hint-text');
+        if (hint) hint.remove();
+    };
+
+    // 검색어 입력 시 실시간 에러 해제
+    const searchWordInput = document.querySelector('input[name="searchWord"]');
+    searchWordInput?.addEventListener('input', function () {
+        if (this.value.trim()) clearSearchWordError(this);
+    });
+
+    // -------------------------------------------------------
+    // 폼 제출 유효성 검사
+    // -------------------------------------------------------
+    const form = document.getElementById('frmSearchDormantMember');
+    form.addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
+        e.preventDefault();
+
+        // 1. 검색어 미입력 검사
+        const searchInput = form.querySelector('input[name="searchWord"]');
+        if (!searchInput.value.trim()) {
+            showSearchWordError(searchInput);
+            searchInput.focus();
+            return;
+        }
+        clearSearchWordError(searchInput);
+
+        // 2. 공급사 구분 '공급사' 선택 시 대상 공급사 미선택 검사
+        const scmFl = form.querySelector('input[name="scmFl"]:checked')?.value;
+        if (scmFl === 'y' && form.querySelectorAll('input[name="scmNo[]"]').length === 0) {
+            NCDSAlert({ message: '공급사를 선택해주세요.', iconType: 'error' });
+            return;
+        }
+
+        // 3. 날짜 유효성 검사
+        const dateInputs = form.querySelectorAll('input[name="orderDt[]"]');
+        const startVal = dateInputs[0]?.value;
+        const endVal   = dateInputs[1]?.value;
+
+        if (startVal && endVal) {
+            const startDate = new Date(startVal);
+            const endDate   = new Date(endVal);
+
+            // 3개월(89일) 초과 검사
+            const diffDays = (endDate - startDate) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24);
+            if (diffDays > 89) {
+                NCDSAlert({ message: '3개월까지 검색하실 수 있습니다.', iconType: 'error' });
+                return;
+            }
+
+            // 최근 5년 이전 검사 (allowInput 우회 최종 방어)
+            if (startDate < fiveYearsAgo) {
+                NCDSAlert({ message: '최근 5년의 내역만 조회하실 수 있습니다.', iconType: 'error' });
+                return;
+            }
+        }
+
+        // 유효성 통과 → 폼 제출
+        form.submit();
+    });
+
+    // -------------------------------------------------------
+    // pageSize 변경 시 폼 제출
+    // -------------------------------------------------------
+    document.getElementById('pageSize')?.addEventListener('change', function () {
+        const pageSizeInput = document.createElement('input');
+        pageSizeInput.type = 'hidden';
+        pageSizeInput.name = 'pageSize';
+        pageSizeInput.value = this.value;
+        form.appendChild(pageSizeInput);
+        form.submit();
+    });
+
+    // -------------------------------------------------------
+    // 초기화 버튼
+    // -------------------------------------------------------
+    // native form.submit() 은 submit 이벤트를 건너뛰어 검색어 필수 검증을 우회하므로 페이지 재진입으로 되돌린다
+    document.querySelector('.js-btn-reset')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
+        location.href = location.pathname;
+    });
+})();
+</script>
```

```diff
                                         <?php } else {?>
                                             <?php if($rowAll === 0){ ?>
                                                 <td <?= $orderGoodsRowSpan ?>>
-                                                    <?php if($data['receiverZonecode']){ echo "[".$data['receiverZonecode']."]"; } ?>
```

```diff
         $this->db->set_insert_db(DB_MEMBER_SLEEP, $arrBind['param'], $arrBind['bind'], 'y');

+    /**
+     * 전일 휴면 전환된 회원 목록(주문 PII 분리 배치용).
+     *
+     * @return array 회원 행 목록([{memNo}])
+     */
+    public function getSleepMembersAtYesterday(): array
+    {
+        $from = date('Y-m-d 00:00:00', strtotime('-1 day'));
+        $to   = date('Y-m-d 23:59:59', strtotime('-1 day'));
+
+        $arrBind = [];
+        $this->db->bind_param_push($arrBind, 's', $from);
+        $this->db->bind_param_push($arrBind, 's', $to);
+        $strSQL = 'SELECT memNo FROM ' . DB_MEMBER_SLEEP . ' WHERE sleepDt BETWEEN ? AND ? GROUP BY memNo';
+
+        return $this->db->secondary()->query_fetch($strSQL, $arrBind);
+    }
+
```

```diff
                         $arrBind = $this->db->get_binding(DBTableField::tableOrderCashReceipt(), $value, 'insert');
                         $arrBind['param'][] = 'sno';
                         $this->db->bind_param_push($arrBind['bind'], 'i', $value['sno']);
```

```diff
+            //   - 대상: 탈퇴·휴면 둘 다(분리보관 완료 플래그로 판정해 일반 주문은 분리보관 조회 생략)
+            $separationType = WithdrawnMemberType::tryFrom($orderData['separationStatus'] ?? '');
+            if ($separationType !== null && is_array($presentReceiver) === true && empty($presentReceiver['sno']) === false) {
+                $presentReceiver = \App::getInstance(WithdrawnOrderHydratorService::class)
+                    ->restore($presentReceiver, WithdrawnSection::PRESENT, $separationType, $presentReceiver['sno']);
+            }
+
             $receiverInfo = $this->getReceiverInfo($orderData, $presentReceiver);
             $recipientInfo = $this->getRecipientInfo($presentReceiver);
             $replaceArguments = $this->getReplaceArguments($orderData, $presentReceiver);
```

```diff
+ * 안심번호
+ *
+ * @author haky <haky2@godo.co.kr>
+ */
+class SafeNumber extends \Component\AbstractComponent
+{
+    // 안심번호 api 통신 모듈
+    private $_safeNumberApi = null;
+
+    /**
+     * 생성자
+     */
+    public function __construct()
+    {
+        parent::__construct();
+        $this->_safeNumberApi = \App::load('Component\\Godo\\GodoSafeNumberServerApi');
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * 안심번호 신청값 반환
+     *
+     * @return bool
+     */
+    public function getSafeNumberFl()
+    {
+        $orderBasic = gd_policy('order.basic');
+
+        if (empty($orderBasic['safeNumberFl']) || $orderBasic['safeNumberFl'] == 'n' || (isset($orderBasic['safeNumberServiceFl']) && $orderBasic['safeNumberServiceFl'] == 'off')) {
+            return false;
+        } else {
+            return true;
+        }
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * 안심번호 설정값 반환
+     *
+     * @return bool
+     */
+    public function getSafeNumberUseFl()
+    {
+        $result = false;
+        $orderBasic = gd_policy('order.basic');
+
+        if (empty($orderBasic['safeNumberFl']) || $orderBasic['safeNumberFl'] == 'n' || (isset($orderBasic['safeNumberServiceFl']) && $orderBasic['safeNumberServiceFl'] == 'off')) {
+            return $result;
+        }
+
+        if ($orderBasic['useSafeNumberFl'] == 'y') {
+            $result = true;
+        }
+
+        return $result;
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * 주문정보 테이블의 안심번호 관련 정보 반환
+     *
+     * @param mixed $orderNo 주문 번호
+     *
+     * @return array
+     */
+    public function getSafeNumberList($orderNo)
+    {
+        $arrBind = [];
+        if (is_array($orderNo)) {
+            $where = 'orderNo IN (' . gd_implode(',', array_fill(0, gd_count($orderNo), '?')) . ')';
+            foreach ($orderNo as $item) {
+                $this->db->bind_param_push($arrBind, 's', $item);
+            }
+        } else {
+            $where = 'orderNo = ?';
+            $this->db->bind_param_push($arrBind, 's', $orderNo);
+        }
+
+        $strSQL = 'SELECT sno, orderNo, receiverCellPhone, receiverUseSafeNumberFl, receiverSafeNumber, receiverSafeNumberDt FROM ' . DB_ORDER_INFO . ' WHERE ' . $where;
+        $safeNumberList = $this->db->query_fetch($strSQL, $arrBind);
+
+        return $this->restoreSeparatedSafeNumberList(is_array($safeNumberList) ? $safeNumberList : []);
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * [휴면 복원]
+     *   - 원복 데이터: 수취인 연락처(receiverCellPhone) · 안심번호(receiverSafeNumber)
+     *   - 용도: 발급/재발급 판정이 두 값의 존재 여부로 갈리므로 분리 전과 동일하게 동작시킨다
+     *   - 대상: 휴면만(탈퇴는 레거시 D+1 배치가 이미 공백 처리하던 baseline 유지. 분리보관 완료 플래그를 일괄 판정해 휴면 분리 주문만 분리보관 조회)
+     *
+     * @param array $safeNumberList getSafeNumberList 조회 결과
+     * @return array 공백이던 행만 분리보관값으로 채운 결과
+     */
+    private function restoreSeparatedSafeNumberList(array $safeNumberList): array
+    {
+        $candidateOrderNos = [];
+        foreach ($safeNumberList as $row) {
+            if (empty($row['orderNo'])) {
+                continue;
+            }
+            if (empty($row['receiverCellPhone']) || empty($row['receiverSafeNumber'])) {
+                $candidateOrderNos[] = $row['orderNo'];
+            }
+        }
+        if (count($candidateOrderNos) === 0) {
+            return $safeNumberList;
+        }
+        $separationStatusMap = \App::getInstance(OrderRepository::class)->findSeparationStatusByOrderNos($candidateOrderNos);
+
+        $hydrator = \App::getInstance(WithdrawnOrderHydratorService::class);
+        foreach ($safeNumberList as $key => $row) {
+            if (empty($row['orderNo']) === true) {
+                continue;
+            }
+            if (($separationStatusMap[$row['orderNo']] ?? '') !== WithdrawnMemberType::SLEEP->value) {
+                continue;
+            }
+            if (empty($row['receiverCellPhone']) === false && empty($row['receiverSafeNumber']) === false) {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            if (empty($row['receiverCellPhone']) === true) {
+                $orderInfo = $hydrator->hydrateOne(WithdrawnSection::ORDER_INFO, WithdrawnMemberType::SLEEP, $row['orderNo']);
+                if (empty($orderInfo['receiverCellPhone']) === false) {
+                    $safeNumberList[$key]['receiverCellPhone'] = $orderInfo['receiverCellPhone'];
+                }
+            }
+            if (empty($row['receiverSafeNumber']) === true) {
+                $safeNumber = $hydrator->hydrateOne(WithdrawnSection::SAFE_NUMBER, WithdrawnMemberType::SLEEP, $row['orderNo']);
+                if (empty($safeNumber['receiverSafeNumber']) === false) {
+                    $safeNumberList[$key]['receiverSafeNumber'] = $safeNumber['receiverSafeNumber'];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        return $safeNumberList;
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * 안심번호 신청
+     *
+     * @param string $orderNo 주문 번호
+     *
+     * @return mixed
+     */
+    public function setUseSafeNumber($orderNo)
+    {
+        if ($this->getSafeNumberUseFl() == false) {
+            return false;
+        }
+
+        $orderInfo = $this->getSafeNumberList($orderNo);
+
+        // 휴대폰번호가 있고 발급대기인 경우
+        $cellPhoneList = [];
+        foreach ($orderInfo as $oKey => $oVal) {
+            if ($oVal['receiverUseSafeNumberFl'] == 'w' && empty($oVal['receiverCellPhone']) == false) {
+                $cellPhoneList[$oVal['sno']] = str_replace('-', '', $oVal['receiverCellPhone']);
+            }
+        }
+
+        if (empty($cellPhoneList) == false && gd_count($cellPhoneList) > 0) {
+            // 안심번호 사용 업데이트
+            $arrUpdate['receiverUseSafeNumberFl'] = 'y';
+            $arrBind = $this->db->get_binding(DBTableField::tableOrderInfo(), $arrUpdate, 'update', gd_array_keys($arrUpdate));
+            if (is_array($cellPhoneList) && gd_count($cellPhoneList) > 1) {
+                $where = 'sno IN (' . gd_implode(',', array_fill(0, gd_count($cellPhoneList), '?')) . ')';
+                foreach ($cellPhoneList as $cKey => $cVal) {
+                    $this->db->bind_param_push($arrBind['bind'], 'i', $cKey);
+                }
+            } else {
+                $where = 'sno = ?';
+                $this->db->bind_param_push($arrBind['bind'], 'i', gd_array_keys($cellPhoneList)[0]);
+            }
+            $this->db->set_update_db(DB_ORDER_INFO, $arrBind['param'], $where, $arrBind['bind']);
+
+            // 안심번호 발급 요청
+            $this->_safeNumberApi->safeNumberAPI('match', $cellPhoneList);
+        }
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * 안심번호 미발급건 재요청 (주문번호)
+     *
+     * @param mixed $orderNo 주문 번호
+     *
+     * @return mixed
+     */
+    public function resetSafeNumberByOrderNo($orderNo)
+    {
+        if ($this->getSafeNumberFl() == false) {
+            return false;
+        }
+
+        $orderInfo = $this->getSafeNumberList($orderNo);
+
+        // 휴대폰번호가 있고 사용상태이지만 안심번호 등록이 되지 않은 경우
+        $cellPhoneList = [];
+        foreach ($orderInfo as $oKey => $oVal) {
+            if ($oVal['receiverUseSafeNumberFl'] == 'y' && empty($oVal['receiverSafeNumber']) && empty($oVal['receiverCellPhone']) == false) {
+                $cellPhoneList[$oVal['sno']] = str_replace('-', '', $oVal['receiverCellPhone']);
+            }
+        }
+
+        if (empty($cellPhoneList) == false && gd_count($cellPhoneList) > 0) {
+            $this->_resetSafeNumber($cellPhoneList);
+        }
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * 안심번호 미발급건 재요청 (주문정보)
+     *
+     * @param array $orderInfo 주문 정보
+     *
+     * @return mixed
+     */
+    public function resetSafeNumberByOrderInfo($orderInfo)
+    {
+        if ($this->getSafeNumberFl() == false) {
+            return false;
+        }
+
+        // 휴대폰번호가 있고 사용상태이지만 안심번호 등록이 되지 않은 경우
+        if (empty($orderInfo['sno']) == false && $orderInfo['receiverUseSafeNumberFl'] == 'y' && empty($orderInfo['receiverSafeNumber']) && empty($orderInfo['receiverCellPhone']) == false) {
+            $orderInfo['receiverCellPhone'] = str_replace('-', '', $orderInfo['receiverCellPhone']);
+            $params[$orderInfo['sno']] = $orderInfo['receiverCellPhone'];
+            $result = $this->_resetSafeNumber($params);
+        } else {
+            $result = false;
+        }
+
+        return $result;
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * 안심번호 미발급건 재요청
+     *
+     * @param array $data 재요청 정보
+     *
+     * @return mixed
+     */
+    private function _resetSafeNumber($data)
+    {
+        $result = $this->_safeNumberApi->safeNumberAPI('rematch', $data);
+
+        $this->db->reconnect(
+            function ($reconnectDatabase) use ($result) {
+                if ($result['result'] == 'success' && empty($result['data']) == false && gd_count($result['data']) > 0) {
+                    foreach ($result['data'] as $sKey => $sVal) {
+                        // [휴면 분리보관] 분리 주문의 안심번호는 분리보관에 암호화 보관하고 원본은 공백을 유지한다(발급일시만 원본 기록)
+                        if ($this->saveSeparatedSafeNumber((int) $sKey, (string) $sVal) === true) {
+                            $arrBind = [];
+                            $reconnectDatabase->bind_param_push($arrBind, 'i', $sKey);
+                            $reconnectDatabase->set_update_db(DB_ORDER_INFO, ['receiverSafeNumberDt = now()'], 'sno = ?', $arrBind);
+                            unset($arrBind);
+                            continue;
+                        }
+
+                        $arrBind = [];
+                        $arrUpdate[] = 'receiverSafeNumber = ?';
+                        $arrUpdate[] = 'receiverSafeNumberDt = now()';
+                        $reconnectDatabase->bind_param_push($arrBind, 's', $sVal);
+                        $reconnectDatabase->bind_param_push($arrBind, 'i', $sKey);
+                        $reconnectDatabase->set_update_db(DB_ORDER_INFO, $arrUpdate, 'sno = ?', $arrBind);
+                        unset($arrBind, $arrUpdate);
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        );
+
+        return $result;
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * 분리 주문(휴면)이면 새 안심번호를 분리보관에 암호화 기록한다.
+     *
+     * 원본(es_orderInfo.receiverSafeNumber)은 공백을 유지해야 분리보관이 무효화되지 않는다.
+     *
+     * @param int    $orderInfoSno es_orderInfo.sno
+     * @param string $safeNumber   발급된 안심번호(평문)
+     * @return bool 분리보관에 기록했으면 true, 분리 주문이 아니면 false(호출부가 원본에 기록)
+     */
+    private function saveSeparatedSafeNumber(int $orderInfoSno, string $safeNumber): bool
+    {
+        if ($safeNumber === '') {
+            return false;
+        }
+
+        $orderNo = $this->getOrderNoByOrderInfoSno($orderInfoSno);
+        if ($orderNo === '') {
+            return false;
+        }
+
+        $sleepOrderRepository = \App::getInstance(MemberSleepOrderRepository::class);
+        if ($sleepOrderRepository->existsByOrderNo($orderNo) === false) {
+            return false;
+        }
+
+        $encryptor = \App::getInstance('encryptor');
+        $encryptor->setCipher(Ciphers::AES_256_CBC);
+
+        return $sleepOrderRepository->updateSafeNumberByOrderNo($orderNo, $encryptor->mysqlAesEncrypt($safeNumber));
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * es_orderInfo.sno 로 주문번호를 조회한다(비 PII 컬럼).
+     *
+     * @return string 없으면 빈 문자열
+     */
+    private function getOrderNoByOrderInfoSno(int $orderInfoSno): string
+    {
+        $arrBind = [];
+        $this->db->bind_param_push($arrBind, 'i', $orderInfoSno);
+        $row = $this->db->query_fetch('SELECT orderNo FROM ' . DB_ORDER_INFO . ' WHERE sno = ?', $arrBind, false);
+
+        return (string) ($row['orderNo'] ?? '');
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * 안심번호 수정
+     *
+     * @param array $arrData 수정 정보
+     *
+     * @return mixed
+     */
+    public function modifySafeNumber($arrData)
+    {
+        if ($this->getSafeNumberFl() == false) {
+            return false;
+        }
+
+        // 안심번호가 없이 수정을 요청할 경우 (ex.발급대기) 재매칭 통신 수행
+        if (empty($arrData['receiverSafeNumber'])) {
+            $arrData['receiverCellPhone'] = $arrData['receiverOldCellPhone'];
+            $resetData = $this->resetSafeNumberByOrderInfo($arrData);
+            if ($resetData['result'] == 'success' && empty($resetData['data']) == false && gd_count($resetData['data']) > 0) {
+                $arrData['receiverSafeNumber'] = $resetData['data'][$arrData['sno']];
+            }
+        }
+
+        if (empty($arrData['receiverSafeNumber']) || empty($arrData['receiverOldCellPhone']) || empty($arrData['receiverNewCellPhone'])) {
+            $result = false;
+        } else {
+            $data['safeNumber'] = str_replace('-', '', $arrData['receiverSafeNumber']);
+            $data['oldNumber'] = str_replace('-', '', $arrData['receiverOldCellPhone']);
+            $data['phoneNumber'] = str_replace('-', '', $arrData['receiverNewCellPhone']);
+
+            $result = $this->_safeNumberApi->safeNumberAPI('modify', $data);
+        }
+
+        return $result;
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * 주문상세페이지 - 안심번호 사용해지
+     *
+     * @param array $data 해지할 안심번호 정보
+     *
+     * @return bool
+     */
+    public function cancelSafeNumber($data)
+    {
+        if ($this->getSafeNumberFl() == false) {
+            return false;
+        }
+
+        $data['safeNumber'] = str_replace('-', '', $data['safeNumber']);
+        $data['phoneNumber'] = str_replace('-', '', $data['phoneNumber']);
+
+        $result = $this->_safeNumberApi->safeNumberAPI('remove', $data);
+
+        if ($result['result'] == 'success') {
+            $updateData = [
+                'receiverUseSafeNumberFl' => 'c'
+            ];
+            $arrBind = $this->db->get_binding(DBTableField::tableOrderInfo(), $updateData, 'update', gd_array_keys($updateData));
+            $this->db->bind_param_push($arrBind['bind'], 'i', $data['sno']);
+            $this->db->set_update_db(DB_ORDER_INFO, $arrBind['param'], 'sno = ?', $arrBind['bind']);
+        }
+
+        return $result;
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * 안심번호 저장
+     *
+     * @param array $data 안심번호
+     *
+     * return bool
+     */
+    public function setSafeNumber($data)
+    {
+        if ($this->getSafeNumberFl() == false) {
+            return false;
+        }
+
+        foreach ($data as $sKey => $sVal) {
+            $arrBind = [];
+            $arrUpdate[] = 'receiverSafeNumber = ?';
+            $arrUpdate[] = 'receiverSafeNumberDt = now()';
+            $this->db->bind_param_push($arrBind, 's', $sVal);
+            $this->db->bind_param_push($arrBind, 'i', $sKey);
+            $this->db->set_update_db(DB_ORDER_INFO, $arrUpdate, 'sno = ?', $arrBind);
+            unset($arrBind, $arrUpdate);
+        }
+    }
 }
\ No newline at end of file
```

```diff
+            $getData = \App::getInstance(OrderListMaskingService::class)->maskOrderList($getData);
```

### Controller/Admin/Order/OrderListWithdrawnMemberController.php

```diff
--- /dev/null
+++ b/Controller/Admin/Order/OrderListWithdrawnMemberController.php
@@ -0,0 +1,117 @@
+<?php
+/*
+ * Copyright (C) 2026 NHN COMMERCE. - All Rights Reserved
+ *
+ * Unauthorized copying or redistribution of this file in source and binary forms via any medium
+ * is strictly prohibited.
+ */
+namespace Bundle\Controller\Admin\Order;
+
+use Request;
+use Component\Member\Manager;
+use Component\Page\Page;
+use Origin\DTO\Privacy\Withdrawn\WithdrawnOrderListSearchDTO;
+use Origin\Service\Privacy\Withdrawn\WithdrawnOrderListService;
+
+/**
+ * 휴면/탈퇴 회원 주문 리스트 컨트롤러
+ */
+class OrderListWithdrawnMemberController extends \Controller\Admin\Controller
+{
+    public function index()
+    {
+        $this->callMenu('order', 'order', 'withdrawn_member');
+
+        // 공급사 계정 게이트: 공급사 로그인 시 공급사 구분 필터/컬럼 미노출 + 본인 공급사로 강제
+        $isProvider = Manager::isProvider();
+        $this->setData('isProvider', $isProvider);
+
+        $searchDto = WithdrawnOrderListSearchDTO::fromRequest(
+            Request::get()->all(),
+            date('Y-m-d'),
+            $isProvider,
+            (int) \Session::get('manager.scmNo')
+        );
+
+        /** @var WithdrawnOrderListService $withdrawnOrderListService */
+        $withdrawnOrderListService = \App::getInstance(WithdrawnOrderListService::class);
+        $mallService = \App::load('Component\\Mall\\Mall');
+        $this->setData('mallTypeOptions', $withdrawnOrderListService->buildMallTypeOptions($mallService->getListByUseMall()));
+
+        // 최초 진입 = 검색 미수행(보안). 주문자는 필수 검색 조건이므로 검색어까지 있어야 조회한다.
+        // Request::getQueryString() 은 쿼리스트링이 비면 '' 가 아니라 null 을 돌려준다.
+        // 공급사를 고르지 않은 '공급사' 검색은 조건 불충분이므로 조회하지 않는다(화면 우회 방어)
+        $isSearched = !empty(Request::getQueryString())
+            && $searchDto->getSearchWord() !== ''
+            && !$searchDto->isSupplierUnselected();
+
+        // 전체 건수는 검색 여부·검색 조건과 무관하게 항상 노출한다.
+        $totalCount = $withdrawnOrderListService->getTotalCount($searchDto);
+
+        $list = [];
+        $searchCount = 0;
+        $pagination = '';
+        $orderDt = $searchDto->getOrderDt();
+        if ($isSearched) {
+            $result = $withdrawnOrderListService->getList($searchDto);
+            $searchCount = $result['total'];
+            $list = $this->buildListRows($result['list']);
+            // 서비스가 보안 클램프한 실제 조회 기간을 화면 datepicker 에 되돌린다
+            $orderDt = $result['orderDt'];
+
+            $pageObj = new Page($searchDto->page, $searchCount, $totalCount, $searchDto->pageSize);
+            $pageObj->setPage();
+            $pageObj->setUrl(Request::getQueryString());
+            $pagination = $pageObj->getPage();
+        }
+
+        $this->setData('data', ['list' => $list]);
+        $this->setData('cnt', ['search' => $searchCount, 'total' => $totalCount]);
+        $this->setData('pagination', $pagination);
+        $this->setData('search', $searchDto->toSearchForm());
+        $this->setData('orderDt', $orderDt);
+        $this->setData('pageSize', $searchDto->pageSize);
+        $this->getView()->setPageName('order/order_list_withdrawn_member.php');
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * 조회 행을 뷰 노출 필드로 화이트리스트한다.
+     *
+     * @param array $rows WithdrawnOrderListService::getList() 의 list
+     * @return array 각 원소: listNo/orderNo/mallTypeStr/scmNm/scmSupplierNames/memberTypeStr/
+     *               memId/orderName/bankSender/orderCellPhone/orderEmail/
+     *               receiverName/receiverCellPhone/receiverAddress/receiverAddressSub/receiverZonecode
+     */
+    protected function buildListRows(array $rows): array
+    {
+        // 이 화면은 분리보관 원본을 복호화해 보여주므로, '개인정보조회 제한' 권한이 걸린 운영자에게는
+        // 식별 가능한 항목을 부분마스킹해 내보낸다(권한이 없으면 masking() 이 원본을 그대로 돌려준다)
+        $memberMasking = \App::load('Component\\Member\\MemberMasking');
+
+        $list = [];
+        foreach ($rows as $row) {
+            $list[] = [
+                'listNo'             => $row['listNo'],
+                'orderNo'            => $row['orderNo'],
+                'mallTypeStr'        => $row['mallTypeStr'],        // 상점 구분(domainFl → 기준/영문/중문/일문)
+                'scmNm'              => $row['scmNm'],              // 공급사 구분 라벨(본사/공급사)
+                'scmSupplierNames'   => $row['scmSupplierNames'],  // 공급사면 주문별 실제 공급사명
+                'memberTypeStr'      => $row['memberTypeStr'],
+                // 주문자 PII(분리보관 복호화)
+                'memId'              => $memberMasking->masking('order', 'id', $row['memId']),
+                'orderName'          => $memberMasking->masking('order', 'name', $row['orderName']),
+                'bankSender'         => $memberMasking->masking('order', 'name', $row['bankSender']),
+                'orderCellPhone'     => $memberMasking->masking('order', 'tel', $row['orderCellPhone']),
+                'orderEmail'         => $memberMasking->masking('order', 'email', $row['orderEmail']),
+                // 수령자 PII(분리보관 복호화) + 우편번호(원본 비PII)
+                'receiverName'       => $memberMasking->masking('order', 'name', $row['receiverName']),
+                'receiverCellPhone'  => $memberMasking->masking('order', 'tel', $row['receiverCellPhone']),
+                'receiverAddress'    => $memberMasking->masking('order', 'address', $row['receiverAddress']),
+                'receiverAddressSub' => $memberMasking->masking('order', 'address', $row['receiverAddressSub']),
+                'receiverZonecode'   => $row['receiverZonecode'],
+            ];
+        }
+
+        return $list;
+    }
+}
```

```diff
 /**
```

### Controller/Admin/Order/OrderViewController.php

```diff
--- a/Controller/Admin/Order/OrderViewController.php
+++ b/Controller/Admin/Order/OrderViewController.php
@@ -26,6 +26,12 @@ use Framework\Utility\DateTimeUtils;
 use Framework\Security\Token;
 use Globals;
 use Origin\Service\Coupon\UpdateCouponService;
+use Origin\Service\Privacy\Masking\ColumnMaskingService;
+use Origin\Service\Privacy\Masking\MaskingTargetService;
+use Origin\Service\Privacy\Masking\OrderDetailMaskingService;
+use Origin\Service\Privacy\Withdrawn\WithdrawnOrderHydratorService;
+use Origin\Enum\Privacy\Withdrawn\WithdrawnMemberType;
+use Origin\Enum\Privacy\Withdrawn\WithdrawnSection;
 use Session;
 use Request;
 
@@ -62,10 +68,35 @@ class OrderViewController extends \Controller\Admin\Controller
             $data = $order->getOrderView($request->get()->get('orderNo'), null, null, null, $excludeStatus, null);
             $data['visitDeliveryInfo'] = $delivery->getVisitDeliveryInfo($data);
 
-            $withdrawnMembersOrderData = $order->getWithdrawnMembersOrderViewByOrderNo($data['orderNo']);
-            $withdrawnMembersPersonalData = $withdrawnMembersOrderData['personalInfo'][0];
-            $withdrawnMembersOrderFl = !empty($withdrawnMembersPersonalData) ? 'y' : 'n';
-            $this->setData('withdrawnMembersOrderFl', $withdrawnMembersOrderFl);
+            $separationStatus = $data['separationStatus'] ?? '';
+            $withdrawnHydrator = \App::getInstance(WithdrawnOrderHydratorService::class);
+
+            $withdrawnMembersPersonalData = null;
+
+            // [휴면 복원]
+            //   - 원복 데이터: 주문자/수령자 PII(orderName·연락처·주소 등)
+            //   - 용도: 주문상세 표기·기능(SMS·안심번호 해지/수정)
+            //   - 대상: 휴면만(탈퇴는 별도키 유지=원복 안 함. 분리보관 완료 플래그로 판정해 일반 주문은 분리보관 조회 생략)
+            if ($separationStatus === WithdrawnMemberType::SLEEP->value) {
+                $sleepType = WithdrawnMemberType::SLEEP;
+                $data = $withdrawnHydrator->restore($data, WithdrawnSection::ORDER_INFO, $sleepType, $data['orderNo']);
+                // 안심번호(receiverSafeNumber): 해지/수정 폼이 $data['receiverSafeNumber'] 를 사용 → 동일 동작 위해 복원
```

```diff
+/**

+ * [관리자 모드] 세금계산서 신청 리스트 페이지
+ *
+ * @author artherot
+ * @version 1.0
+ * @since 1.0
+ * @copyright ⓒ 2016, NHN godo: Corp.
+ */
+class TaxInvoiceRequestController extends \Controller\Admin\Controller
+{
+    /**
+     * {@inheritdoc}
+     *
+     * @author Jong-tae Ahn <qnibus@godo.co.kr>
+     */
+    public function index()
+    {
+        // --- 메뉴 설정
+        $this->callMenu('order', 'taxInvoice', 'request');
+
+        // --- 모듈 호출
+        $tax = \App::load('\\Component\\Order\\Tax');
+        $order = \App::load('\\Component\\Order\\OrderAdmin');
+
+
+        // --- 세금계산서 데이터
+        try {
+            $searchDateFl = gd_isset(Request::get()->get('searchDateFl'), 'regDt');
+            Request::get()->set('searchDateFl',$searchDateFl);
+            Request::get()->set('statusFl','r');
+            $getData = $tax->getListTaxInvoice();
+            $page = \App::load('\\Component\\Page\\Page'); // 페이지 재설정
+
+            // 세금계산서 사용여부
+            $taxInfo = $tax->getTaxConf();
+
+        } catch (Exception $e) {
+
+        }
+
+        // --- 관리자 디자인 템플릿
+        $this->setData('data', \App::getInstance(TaxInvoiceListMaskingService::class)->maskRequestList($getData['data']));
+        $this->setData('search', $getData['search']);
+        $this->setData('searchKindASelectBox', \Component\Member\Member::getSearchKindASelectBox());
+        $this->setData('sort', $getData['sort']);
+        $this->setData('checked', $checked = $getData['checked']);
+        $this->setData('page', $page);
+        $this->setData('taxInfo', $taxInfo);
+        $this->setData('tax', $tax);
+        $this->setData('statusSearchableRange', $order->getOrderStatusAdmin());
+    }
+}
```

### Job/MemberSleepOrderJob.php

```diff
--- /dev/null
+++ b/Job/MemberSleepOrderJob.php
@@ -0,0 +1,127 @@
+<?php
+/**
+ * This is commercial software, only users who have purchased a valid license
+ * and accept to the terms of the License Agreement can install and use this
+ * program.
+ *
+ * Do not edit or add to this file if you wish to upgrade Godomall to newer
+ * versions in the future.
+ *
+ * @copyright ⓒ 2026, NHN COMMERCE Corp.
+ * @link      https://www.nhn-commerce.com/
+ */
+
+namespace Bundle\Job;
+
+use Framework\Application\Bootstrap\Log;
+use Framework\Command\JobScheduler\AbstractJob;
+use Origin\Enum\Privacy\Withdrawn\WithdrawnMemberType;
+use Origin\Service\Privacy\Withdrawn\WithdrawnMemberOrderSeparatorService;
+use Exception;
+use Origin\Service\Privacy\Withdrawn\WithdrawnJobRunningFlagService;
+
+/**
+ * 휴면회원 주문내역 분리(전일 전환분 D+1 보정)
+ * 전환 즉시 분리에서 누락된 건을 하루 뒤 보정한다.
+ *
+ * @usage   setCronSchedule 설정 사용
+ * -    -    -    -    -    -
+ * |    |    |    |    |    |
+ * |    |    |    |    |    + year [optional]
+ * |    |    |    |    +----- day of week (0 - 7) (Sunday=0 or 7)
+ * |    |    |    +---------- month (1 - 12)
+ * |    |    +--------------- day of month (1 - 31)
+ * |    +-------------------- hour (0 - 23)
+ * +------------------------- min (0 - 59)
+ *
+ * @package Godo_Job_Schedule
+ */
+class MemberSleepOrderJob extends AbstractJob
+{
+    /**
+     * Job 실행 전 처리 로직
+     *
+     * @param object $config JobConfig 객체
+     */
+    protected function setup($config)
+    {
+        $config
+            ->setCronSchedule('0 3 * * *')
+            ->setName('member_sleep_order')
+            ->setIgnoreUserAbort(true)
+            ->setMaxExecuteTime(0)
+            ->setMaxMemoryLimit('-1')
+            ->setRangeExecuteTime(null)
+            ->setDevelopment(true)
+            ->setSendGms(false)
+            ->setForceRun(false);
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * Job 실행
+     *
+     * @return boolean 실행 성공 여부
+     */
+    protected function execute()
+    {
+        // 분리보관 이관이 이 Job 실행 중에는 비켜 가도록 실행 표시를 남긴다(이관 종료 후 제거)
+        (new WithdrawnJobRunningFlagService())->markRunningUntilExit(WithdrawnJobRunningFlagService::JOB_KEY_SLEEP_ORDER);
+        $logger = \App::getInstance('logger')->channel(Log::CHANNEL_SCHEDULER);
+        $logger->info(sprintf('Execute job name is %s.', __CLASS__));
+
+        try {
+            /** @var \Bundle\Component\Member\Sleep\SleepService $sleepService */
+            $sleepService = \App::load('Component\\Member\\Sleep\\SleepService');
+            $separator = \App::getInstance(WithdrawnMemberOrderSeparatorService::class);
+
+            $members = $sleepService->getSleepMembersAtYesterday();
+            foreach ((array) $members as $member) {
+                $memNo = (int) $member['memNo'];
+                $separator->separate($memNo, WithdrawnMemberType::SLEEP);
+                $logger->info(sprintf('Separating order PII of sleep member : memNo [%s]', $memNo));
+            }
+
+            $logger->info(__CLASS__ . ', end');
+            return true;
+        } catch (Exception $e) {
+            $logger->error(__METHOD__ . ', ' . $e->getFile() . '[' . $e->getLine() . '], ' . $e->getMessage(), $e->getTrace());
+            return false;
+        }
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * @param boolean $isSuccess
+     */
+    protected function complete($isSuccess)
+    {
+        if ($isSuccess) {
+            $this->logMessage('OK', __CLASS__ . '  job is success!');
+        } else {
+            $this->logMessage('FAIL', __CLASS__ . 'this job is failure!');
+        }
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * Shutdown 예외처리
+     *
+     * @param string $code    시스템 다운 코드
+     * @param string $message 시스템 다운 메시지
+     */
+    protected function shutdown($code, $message)
+    {
+        switch ($code) {
+            case self::SHUTDOWN_NOT_ENOUGH_MEMORY:
+                break;
+            case self::SHUTDOWN_CONNECTION_TIMEOUT:
+                break;
+            case self::SHUTDOWN_CONNECTION_ABORTED:
+                break;
+            case self::SHUTDOWN_CONNECTION_TIMEOUT_ABORTED:
+                break;
+            case self::SHUTDOWN_CONNECTION_UNKNOWN_ERROR:
+                break;
+            default:
+                break;
+        }
+    }
+}
```

```diff
--- a/Repository/Order/OrderGoodsRepository.php
+++ b/Repository/Order/OrderGoodsRepository.php
@@ -14,6 +14,7 @@ use Origin\Enum\Order\EpostReturnInvoiceStatus;
 use Origin\Enum\Order\OrderChannel;
 use Origin\Enum\Order\OrderStatusCode;
 use Origin\Model\Order\OrderGoods;
+use Origin\Service\Privacy\Withdrawn\WithdrawnOrderSearchService;
 
 /**
  * 주문상품(es_orderGoods) 조회
@@ -173,8 +174,23 @@ class OrderGoodsRepository
 
         if ($search->isLikeMatch()) {
             $query->where($column, 'LIKE', '%' . addcslashes($search->getKeyword(), '%_\\') . '%');
-        } else {
+
+            return;
+        }
+
+        // 분리보관된 주문은 원본 컬럼이 공백이라 평문 정확일치로 찾히지 않아, 암호문에서 찾은 주문번호를 함께 건다
+        $withdrawnOrderNos = \App::getInstance(WithdrawnOrderSearchService::class)
+            ->findOrderNosByOrderSearchKey($column, $search->getKeyword());
+
+        if (empty($withdrawnOrderNos)) {
             $query->where($column, $search->getKeyword());
+
+            return;
         }
+
+        $query->where(function (Builder $where) use ($column, $search, $withdrawnOrderNos) {
+            $where->where($column, $search->getKeyword())
+                ->orWhereIn('og.orderNo', $withdrawnOrderNos);
+        });
     }
 }
```
